system://tool-families.md content:
# Tool Families Overview

> [!TIP]
> - Tools in TurboStar are organized into tool families. `base` tools are always active.
> - If a tool belongs to an inactive family, you must call `activate_tool_family(family_name="<family>")` before executing it.
> - To inspect member tools and activation guidance for a specific family, read `system://tool-families/<family>.md`.

| Tool Family | Default Status | Activation Reason | Family Documentation |
| ----------- | -------------- | ----------------- | -------------------- |
| `base` | **Active** | Always active core system tools. | [system://tool-families/base.md](system://tool-families/base.md) |
| `code_review` | *Inactive* | Activate to inspect, confirm, update, or resolve code review findings and security audit items (auto-activates when review items exist) | [system://tool-families/code_review.md](system://tool-families/code_review.md) |
| `editor` | *Inactive* | Activate when running in interactive TurboStar editor UI mode to manage UI error highlighting, status bar messages, and editor tabs | [system://tool-families/editor.md](system://tool-families/editor.md) |
| `git` | *Inactive* | Activate when performing git operations (add, commit, status, diff, log, branch, pull, push, restore) | [system://tool-families/git.md](system://tool-families/git.md) |

system://tool-families/git.md content:
# Tool Family: `git`

- **Activation Reason**: Activate when performing git operations (add, commit, status, diff, log, branch, pull, push, restore)
- **Activation Status**: *Inactive* (Run `activate_tool_family(family_name="git")` to enable)
- **Activation Command**: `activate_tool_family(family_name="git")`

## Guidance & Overview
The 'git' tool family contains tools to interact with Git version control.

Key Tools:
- git_status: Inspect files changed in the workspace.
- git_diff_unstaged / git_diff_staged: Inspect file diffs.
- git_add: Stage changes.
- git_commit: Commit staged changes.
- git_blame: View commit-level line history.

## Member Tools in `git` Family

| Tool Name | Description | Detailed Schema Link |
| --------- | ----------- | -------------------- |
*No static tools explicitly declared under family 'git'.*
